\overset{\circ}{X}_{\lambda_{0}}\cap\cdots\cap\overset{\circ}{X}_{\lambda_{k}}

contains
P
.
Then the set

E:=\{\overset{\circ}{X}_{\lambda_{0}},\ldots,\overset{\circ}{X}_{\lambda_{k}}\}

gives an abelian sheaf

\varpi_{\lambda_{0}\cdots\lambda_{k}{\rm zar}}(\overset{\circ}{X}/\overset{\circ}{S}):=\bigwedge^{k+1}{\mathbb{Z}}^{E}_{\overset{\circ}{X}_{\lambda_{0}}\cap\cdots\cap\overset{\circ}{X}_{\lambda_{k}}}

on a local neighborhood of
P
in
\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(k)}
.
The sheaf

\varpi_{\lambda_{0}\cdots\lambda_{k}{\rm zar}}(\overset{\circ}{X}/\overset{\circ}{S})

is globalized on
\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(k)}
;
we denote this globalized abelian
sheaf by the same symbol

\varpi_{\lambda_{0}\cdots\lambda_{k}{\rm zar}}(\overset{\circ}{X}/\overset{\circ}{S})
.
We denote a local
section of

\varpi_{\lambda_{0}\cdots\lambda_{k}{\rm zar}}(\overset{\circ}{X}/\overset{\circ}{S})

by the following way:

m(\lambda_{0}\cdots\lambda_{k})

(m\in{\mathbb{Z}})
.
Set
\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{X}/\overset{\circ}{S}):=\bigoplus_{\{\lambda_{0},\ldots\lambda_{k}\}}\varpi_{\lambda_{0}\cdots\lambda_{k}{\rm zar}}(\overset{\circ}{X}/\overset{\circ}{S})
.
Set
\varpi^{(0)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{X}/\overset{\circ}{S}):={\mathbb{Z}}_{\overset{\circ}{X}}
.

Let
k
be a nonnegative integer.
Let
Q
be a point of
\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k)}
.
Let
\overset{\circ}{D}_{\mu_{1}},\ldots,\overset{\circ}{D}_{\mu_{k}}

be different smooth components of
\overset{\circ}{D}
such that

\overset{\circ}{D}_{\mu_{1}}\cap\cdots\cap\overset{\circ}{D}_{\mu_{k}}

contains
Q
.
Then the set

F:=\{\overset{\circ}{D}_{\mu_{1}},\ldots,\overset{\circ}{D}_{\mu_{k}}\}

gives an abelian sheaf

\varpi_{\mu_{1}\cdots\mu_{k}{\rm zar}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}):=\bigwedge^{k}{\mathbb{Z}}^{F}_{\overset{\circ}{D}_{\mu_{1}}\cap\cdots\cap\overset{\circ}{D}_{\mu_{k}}}

on a local neighborhood of
Q
in
\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k)}
.
The sheaf

\varpi_{\mu_{1}\cdots\mu_{k}{\rm zar}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})

is globalized on
\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k)}
.
By using
\varpi_{\mu_{1}\cdots\mu_{k}{\rm zar}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})
,
we have an analogous orientation sheaf

\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}):=\bigoplus_{\{\mu_{1},\ldots\mu_{k}\}}\varpi_{\mu_{1}\cdots\mu_{k}{\rm zar}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})

in
\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k)}_{\rm zar}

for
k\in{\mathbb{Z}}_{\geq 0}
.
Set

\varpi^{(k),(k^{\prime})}_{\rm zar}((\overset{\circ}{X},\overset{\circ}{D})/\overset{\circ}{S}):=\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{X}/\overset{\circ}{S})|_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(k)}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k^{\prime})}}\otimes\varpi^{(k^{\prime})}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})|_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(k)}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k^{\prime})}}
.
The orientation sheaves

\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{X}/\overset{\circ}{S})
,

\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})
and

\varpi^{(k),(k^{\prime})}_{\rm zar}((\overset{\circ}{X},\overset{\circ}{D})/\overset{\circ}{S}))

are non-canonically isomorphic to

{\mathbb{Z}}_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(k)}}
,
{\mathbb{Z}}_{\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k)}}

and

{\mathbb{Z}}_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(k)}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k^{\prime})}}
, respectively.
Let
a^{(k)}\colon\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(k)}\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{X}
,

c^{(k)}\colon\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k)}\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{X}
and

a^{(k),(k^{\prime})}\colon\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(k)}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k^{\prime})}\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{X}
be natural morphisms of schemes.
(We do not use a symbol
b^{(k)}
because we use the symbol

b^{(k)}
for another morphism in the
p
-adic case in
[
Nakk6
]
.)
Let
M_{X}
be the log structure of
X
in
\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et}
.
The orientation sheaves

\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{X}/\overset{\circ}{S})
,

\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm zar}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})
and

\varpi^{(k),(k^{\prime})}_{\rm zar}((\overset{\circ}{X},\overset{\circ}{D})/\overset{\circ}{S})

define étale sheaves

\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{X}/\overset{\circ}{S})
,

\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})
and

\varpi^{(k),(k^{\prime})}_{\rm et}((\overset{\circ}{X},\overset{\circ}{D})/\overset{\circ}{S})

in
(\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(k)})_{\rm et}
,

(\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k)})_{\rm et}
,

(\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(k)}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k^{\prime})})_{\rm et}
, respectively.
We have the following canonical isomorphisms

(9.1.1)

a^{(k)}_{{\rm et}*}(\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{X}/\overset{\circ}{S}))\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}\bigwedge^{k+1}(M_{X}^{\rm gp}/{\cal O}_{X}^{*}),

(9.1.2)

c^{(k)}_{{\rm et}*}(\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}))\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}\bigwedge^{k}(M(D)^{\rm gp}/{\cal O}_{X}^{*})

and

(9.1.3)

a^{(k),(k^{\prime})}_{{\rm et}*}(\varpi^{(k),(k^{\prime})}_{\rm et}((\overset{\circ}{X},\overset{\circ}{D})/\overset{\circ}{S}))\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}\bigwedge^{k+1}(M_{X}^{\rm gp}/{\cal O}_{X}^{*})\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\bigwedge^{k^{\prime}}(M(D)^{\rm gp}/{\cal O}_{X}^{*})

as abelian sheaves in
\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et}
.
We have a canonical isomorphism

(9.1.4)

a^{(k),(k^{\prime})}_{{\rm et}*}(\varpi^{(k),(k^{\prime})}_{\rm et}((\overset{\circ}{X},\overset{\circ}{D})/\overset{\circ}{S}))\overset{\sim}{\longleftarrow}a^{(k)}_{{\rm et}*}(\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{X}/\overset{\circ}{S}))\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}c^{(k)}_{{\rm et}*}(\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})).

Henceforth, we denote

a^{(k)}_{{\rm et}*}
,
c^{(k)}_{{\rm et}*}

and
a^{(k),(k^{\prime})}_{{\rm et}*}
simply by

a^{(k)}_{*}
,
c^{(k)}_{*}

and
a^{(k),(k^{\prime})}_{*}
, respectively.
We apply the same rule for

a^{(k)*}_{\rm et}
,
c^{(k)*}_{\rm et}

and
a^{(k),(k^{\prime})*}_{\rm et}
.

Set
(X,D):=X\times_{\overset{\circ}{X}}(\overset{\circ}{X},M(D))
as in
[
NY
, §6]
and

(\overset{\circ}{X},\overset{\circ}{D}):=(\overset{\circ}{X},M(D))
.
Let

\epsilon_{D}\colon(X,D)\longrightarrow X,\quad\epsilon_{X}\colon X\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{X}\quad\text{and}\quad\overset{\circ}{\epsilon}_{D}\colon(\overset{\circ}{X},\overset{\circ}{D})\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{X}

be natural morphisms of log schemes forgetting log structures.
Set

{\epsilon}_{(X,D)}:=\epsilon_{X}\circ\epsilon_{D}
.
The morphisms above induce
the following morphisms of topoi:

\epsilon_{D}\colon(X,D)_{\rm ket}\longrightarrow X_{\rm ket},\quad\epsilon_{X}\colon X_{\rm ket}\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},

{\epsilon}_{(X,D)}:={\epsilon}_{X}\circ{\epsilon}_{D}\colon(X,D)_{\rm ket}\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et}\quad\text{and}\quad\overset{\circ}{\epsilon}_{D}\colon(\overset{\circ}{X},\overset{\circ}{D})_{\rm et}\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et}.

Following
[
Nak3
]
, set
\overset{\circ}{S}_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}:=\overset{\circ}{S}\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}[{\mathbb{N}}]}{\mathbb{Z}}[(l^{m})^{-1}{\mathbb{N}}]
.
(In [loc. cit.] C. Nakayama has used the symbol

{\mathbb{N}}^{1/l^{m}}
instead of
(l^{m})^{-1}{\mathbb{N}}
in
{\mathbb{Q}}
.)
The inclusion morphism

(l^{m})^{-1}{\mathbb{N}}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}{\mathbb{Z}}[(l^{m})^{-1}{\mathbb{N}}]
gives an
fs log structure on
\overset{\circ}{S}_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}
.
Set
S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}:=\varprojlim_{m}S_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}
.
Set also
X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}:=X\times_{S}S_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}
and

X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}:=\varprojlim_{m}X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}
.
Then we have the following natural morphisms of log schemes:

\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}}\colon X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}\longrightarrow X\quad\text{and}\quad\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}\colon X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}\longrightarrow X.

Denote the group scheme

\mu_{l^{m}}:=\underline{\rm Spec}_{\overset{\circ}{S}}({\cal O}_{S}[T_{m}]/(T^{l^{m}}_{m}-1))

over
\overset{\circ}{S}
by
{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{m}(1)
.
There exists a natural morphism

\mu_{l^{m+1}}\longrightarrow\mu_{l^{m}}
defined by
T_{m}\longmapsto T_{m+1}^{l}
.
Set
{\mathbb{Z}}_{l}(1):=\varprojlim_{n}{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{m}(1)
.
The group scheme
{\mathbb{Z}}_{l}(1)=\varprojlim_{m}\mu_{l^{m}}

acts naturally on
S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}
:

(9.1.5)

\displaystyle(\zeta_{l^{m}})_{m\geq 1}\cdot(1\otimes\dfrac{1}{l^{m}}):=\zeta_{l^{m}}\otimes\dfrac{1}{l^{m}}.

Here
(\zeta_{l^{m}})_{m\geq 1}(=(T_{m})_{m\geq 1})
in
the left hand side is an element of
{\mathbb{Z}}_{l}(1)
and

\zeta_{l^{m}}(=(T_{m})_{m\geq 1})
in the right hand side is the image of

\zeta_{l^{m}}\in{\mathbb{Z}}[l^{-1},\zeta_{l^{\infty}}~|~\zeta_{l^{\infty}}\in\mu_{l^{\infty}}]
by
the pull-back of the morphism

S\longrightarrow{\rm Spec}({\mathbb{Z}}[l^{-1},\zeta_{l^{\infty}}~|~\zeta_{l^{\infty}}\in\mu_{l^{\infty}}])
.
This action of
{\mathbb{Z}}_{l}(1)
on
S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}
induces the action
of
{\mathbb{Z}}_{l}(1)
on
X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}
.
For a sheaf
F
in
X_{\rm ket}
,

{\mathbb{Z}}_{l}(1)
acts on

\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}*}\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}^{*}}(F)
since

{\mathbb{Z}}_{l}(1)
acts on
X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}
.
The group schemes
{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{m}(1)
and

{\mathbb{Z}}_{l}(1)=\varprojlim_{m}\mu_{l^{m}}
define abelian sheaves in

\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et}
.
Set

M_{(X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}})}:=M_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}}\oplus_{{\cal O}^{*}_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}}}\overset{\circ}{\pi}{}^{*}_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}}(M(D))

and

M_{(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})}:=M_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}\oplus_{{\cal O}^{*}_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}}\overset{\circ}{\pi}{}^{*}_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}(M(D)).

Denote
(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}},M_{(X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}})})
by

(X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}})

and

(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},M_{(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})})

by
(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})
.
We also have the following natural morphisms of log schemes:

\pi_{(X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}})}\colon(X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}})\longrightarrow(X,D)\quad\text{and}\quad\pi_{(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})}\colon(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\longrightarrow(X,D).

By using the trivial action of
{\mathbb{Z}}_{l}(1)

on
(\overset{\circ}{X},\overset{\circ}{D})
,
we also have a natural action of
{\mathbb{Z}}_{l}(1)
on

(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})
.
Let
I^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{D},l^{n}}

(n\in{\mathbb{N}})

be an injective resolution of
{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}
in
(\overset{\circ}{X},\overset{\circ}{D})_{\rm ket}
.
Then we have a projective system

\{I^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{D},l^{n}}\}_{n=1}^{\infty}
fitting into the following commutative diagram

(9.1.6)

\begin{CD}{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n+1}@>{}>{}>I^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{D},l^{n+1}}\\
@V{{\rm proj}.}V{}V@V{}V{}V\\
{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}@>{}>{}>I^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{D},l^{n}}.\end{CD}

The following non-difficult proposition is important in this paper.

Proposition 9.2
.

There exists a projective system

\{(M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}),Q)\}_{n=1}^{\infty}

of bounded filtered flat resolutions
(
[
NS
, (1.1.17) (2)]
)
of the complexes

(\overset{\circ}{\epsilon}_{D*}(I^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{D},l^{n}}),\tau)
’s in
\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et}
.

Proof.

Let
k
be an integer.
Because the rank of
\overline{M}(D):=M(D)/{\cal O}_{X}^{*}
is finite
(since
\overset{\circ}{X}
is quasi-compact) and
because
\overset{\circ}{\epsilon}_{D}
is proper,

\displaystyle{\rm gr}^{\tau}_{k}(\overset{\circ}{\epsilon}_{D*}(I^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{D},l^{n}}))

\displaystyle=R^{k}\overset{\circ}{\epsilon}_{D*}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})[-k]=0

for
k>>0
which depends only on the relative dimension of
X/S
and
the rank of
\overline{M}(D)
by
[
Nak4
, (7.2) (1)]
.
One may prove this vanishing in the following way in this case by
[
KN
, (2.4)]
.

Consider the following exact sequence

\displaystyle 0\longrightarrow{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{m}(1)\longrightarrow M^{\rm gp}(D)\overset{l^{m}}{\longrightarrow}M^{\rm gp}(D)\longrightarrow 0.

By
[
KN
, (2.4)]
we have

(9.2.1)

\displaystyle{\rm gr}^{\tau}_{k}(\overset{\circ}{\epsilon}_{D*}(I^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{D},l^{n}}))

\displaystyle=R^{k}\overset{\circ}{\epsilon}_{D*}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})[-k]=\bigwedge^{k}(M(D)^{\rm gp}/{\cal O}_{X}^{*})\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}(-k)[-k]

\displaystyle=c^{(k)}_{*}(\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}))\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}(-k)[-k].

Because
\overset{\circ}{X}
is quasi-compact, there exists a positive integer
k_{0}
such that

\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})=0
for
any
k>k_{0}
.
Hence, if
k>k_{0}
, then

R^{k}\overset{\circ}{\epsilon}_{D*}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})=0
.
Consequently there exists a bounded above flat complex

M^{\prime\bullet}_{l^{n}}
of
{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}
-modules in
\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et}

with a filtered quasi-isomorphism

(M^{\prime\bullet}_{l^{n}},\tau)\longrightarrow(\overset{\circ}{\epsilon}_{D*}(I^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{D},l^{n}}),\tau)

(cf.
[
NS
, (1.1.18)]
).
In fact, we can take the projective system

\{M^{\prime\bullet}_{l^{n}}\}_{n=1}^{\infty}

fitting into the following commutative diagram

\begin{CD}(M^{\prime\bullet}_{l^{n+1}},\tau)@>{}>{}>(\overset{\circ}{\epsilon}_{D*}(I^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{D},l^{n+1}}),\tau)\\
@V{}V{}V@V{}V{}V\\
(M^{\prime\bullet}_{l^{n}},\tau)@>{}>{}>(\overset{\circ}{\epsilon}_{D*}(I^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{D},l^{n}}),\tau)\end{CD}

by the dual argument of the proof of
[
NS
, (1.1.8)]
.

We claim that there exists an integer
q_{0}
such that

\displaystyle{\cal T}{\it or}_{q}^{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{\epsilon}_{D*}(I^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{D},l^{n}}),G):={\cal H}^{-q}(\overset{\circ}{\epsilon}_{D*}(I^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{D},l^{n}})\otimes^{L}_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}}G)={\cal H}^{-q}(M^{\prime\bullet}_{l^{n}}\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}}G)=0

for any
q>q_{0}

and for any
{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}
-module

G
in
\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et}
.
Let
q_{1}
be an integer such
that
M^{\prime q}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})=0
for any
q>q_{1}
.
Obviously
\tau_{q_{1}}M^{\prime\bullet}_{l^{n}}=M^{\prime\bullet}_{l^{n}}
.
Consider the following exact sequence

0\longrightarrow\tau_{q_{1}-1}M^{\prime\bullet}_{l^{n}}\longrightarrow\tau_{q_{1}}M^{\prime\bullet}_{l^{n}}\longrightarrow{\rm gr}^{\tau}_{q_{1}}M^{\prime\bullet}_{l^{n}}\longrightarrow 0.

By (
9.2.1
),

{\cal T}{\it or}_{q}^{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}}(\tau_{q_{1}}\overset{\circ}{\epsilon}_{D*}(I^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{D},l^{n}}),G)={\cal T}{\it or}_{q}^{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}}(\tau_{q_{1}-1}\overset{\circ}{\epsilon}_{D*}(I^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{D},l^{n}}),G)

except for finitely many
q
’s. Making this argument repeatedly until
\tau_{0}
,
we see that

{\cal T}{\it or}_{q}^{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{\epsilon}_{D*}(I^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{D},l^{n}}),G)=0
except for finitely many
q
’s. Hence our claim holds.
By the same argument as that of
[
H
, II (4.2)]
,
there exists a bounded complex

M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})

of flat
{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}
-modules which is isomorphic to

\overset{\circ}{\epsilon}_{D*}(I^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{D},l^{n}})
.
By (
9.2.1
) again,
we see that

(M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}),\tau)

is a filtered flat resolution of

(\overset{\circ}{\epsilon}_{D*}(I^{\bullet}_{\overset{\circ}{D},l^{n}}).\tau)
.
∎

Definition 9.3
.

Let
({\cal T},{\cal A})
be a ringed topos. Let
m\leq 2
and
n
be positive integers.
Let
(K^{\bullet},\{P^{(i)}\}_{i=1}^{m})
be an
m
-filtered complex
of
{\cal A}
-modules.

(1) We say that the cohomological sheaves of
(K^{\bullet},\{P^{(i)}\}_{i=1}^{m})
are
constructible
if

{\cal H}^{q}(K^{\bullet})
and
{\cal H}^{q}((P^{(1)}_{k_{1}}\cap P^{(m)}_{k_{m}})K^{\bullet})

(\forall q,\forall k_{1},\forall k_{m}\in{\mathbb{Z}})
are constructible.

(2) We say that
(K^{\bullet},\{P^{(i)}\}_{i=1}^{m})
has
finite tor-dimension
if the

{\cal A}
-modules

K^{\bullet}
and
K^{\bullet}/(P^{(1)}_{k_{1}}\cap P^{(m)}_{k_{m}})K^{\bullet}

(\forall k_{1},\forall k_{m}\in{\mathbb{Z}})

have finite tor-dimension.

In the following let
m
be a positive integer less than or equal to
2
.

Proposition 9.4
.

Assume that the filtrations
P^{(1)}
and
P^{(m)}
are biregular.
Then the following hold
:

(1)
The cohomological sheaves of
(K^{\bullet},\{P^{(i)}\}_{i=1}^{m})
are constructible
if and only if
the cohomological sheaves of

{\rm gr}^{P^{(1)}}_{k_{1}}{\rm gr}^{P^{(m)}}_{k_{m}}K^{\bullet}
is constructible
for any
k_{1},k_{m}\in{\mathbb{Z}}
.

(2)
The filtered complex
(K^{\bullet},\{P^{(i)}\}_{i=1}^{m})
has finite tor-dimension
if and only if
{\rm gr}^{P^{(1)}}_{k_{1}}{\rm gr}^{P^{(m)}}_{k_{m}}K^{\bullet}
has finite tor-dimension
for any
k_{1},k_{m}\in{\mathbb{Z}}
.

Proof.

(1): By
[
SGA 4-3
, IX (2.6) (ii)]
, the constructivility is stable
under the extension of an exact sequence
of
{\cal A}
-modules.
By this fact and the easy argument in the proof of (
2.13
), we obtain (1).

(2): By the definition of the finite tor-dimension, the property of the finite tor-dimension is
stable under the extension of an exact sequence of
{\cal A}
-modules.
By this fact and the easy arguments in the proof of (
2.8
) and (
2.13
),
we obtain (2).
∎

Definition 9.5
.

Let
({\cal T},{\cal A})
be a ringed topos.

(1) Let
{\rm D}{\rm F}^{m}({\cal T},{\cal A})
be the derived category of

m
-filtered complexes of
{\cal A}
-modules.
Let
{\rm D}^{\rm b}{\rm F}^{m}_{\rm ctf}({\cal T},{\cal A})
be the full-subcategory of
the derived category of bounded filtered complexes whose objects

(K^{\bullet},\{P_{k}\}_{k=1}^{m})
’s have finite tor-dimension and
whose cohomological sheaves are constructible.

(2) Assume that
{\cal A}
is the projective limit
{\cal A}=\varprojlim_{n\in{\mathbb{N}}}{\cal A}_{n}
for
sheaves of commutative rings with unit elements in
{\cal T}
.
Let
{\rm D}^{\rm b}{\rm F}_{\rm ctf}({\cal T},{\cal A})
be the projective 2-limit of

{\rm D}^{\rm b}{\rm F}_{\rm ctf}({\cal T},{\cal A}_{n})
’s:
an object of
{\rm D}^{\rm b}{\rm F}_{\rm ctf}({\cal T},{\cal A})

is a projective system

\{(K^{\bullet}_{n},\{P_{k}\}_{k=1}^{m})\}_{n=0}^{\infty}
,
where
(K^{\bullet}_{n},\{P_{k}\}_{k=1}^{m})
is
an object of
{\rm D}^{\rm b}{\rm F}_{\rm ctf}({\cal T},{\cal A}_{n})

such that

(K^{\bullet}_{n+1},\{P_{k}\}_{k=1}^{m})\otimes^{L}_{{\cal A}_{n+1}}{\cal A}_{n}=(K^{\bullet}_{n},\{P_{k}\}_{k=1}^{m})\quad(\forall n\in{\mathbb{N}})

in
{\rm D}^{\rm b}{\rm F}_{\rm ctf}({\cal T},{\cal A}_{n})
.
Here
\otimes^{L}_{{\cal A}_{n+1}}
is the derived tensor product of bounded below

m
-filtered complexes defined in (
6.2.8
) (cf.
[
NS
, (1.2.5.7)]
).
A morphism in
{\rm D}^{\rm b}{\rm F}^{m}_{\rm ctf}({\cal T},{\cal A})

is obviously defined.

In the case
m=1
, we denote
{\rm D}^{\rm b}{\rm F}^{m}_{\rm ctf}

by simply
{\rm D}^{\rm b}{\rm F}_{\rm ctf}
.

Corollary 9.6
.

The filtered complexes

(R\overset{\circ}{\epsilon}_{D*}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}),\tau)\in{\rm D}^{+}{\rm F}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
’s in

\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et}
define an object

(R\overset{\circ}{\epsilon}_{D*}({\mathbb{Z}}_{l}),\tau)
of

{\rm D}^{\rm b}{\rm F}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}_{l})
.

Proof.

By the definition of
(M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}),Q)
and
by (
9.2.1
), we have the following formula:

(9.6.1)

{\rm gr}_{k}^{Q}M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})={\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}(-k)\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}c^{(k)}_{*}(\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}))[-k]

in
D^{\rm b}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
.
Hence the cohomological sheaves of the graded complexes of

(M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}),Q)
are flat and smooth.
We have to prove that the natural morphism

(M^{\bullet}_{l^{n+1}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}),Q)\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n+1}}{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}\longrightarrow(M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}),Q)

is a quasi-isomorphism.
Because
(M^{\bullet}_{l^{n+1}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}),Q)

is a bounded above filtered flat complex and the cohomological sheaves of

{\rm gr}^{Q}_{k}M^{\bullet}_{l^{n+1}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})
are flat,

(9.6.2)

\displaystyle{\cal H}^{q}({\rm gr}^{Q}_{k}(M^{\bullet}_{l^{n+1}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n+1}}{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}))

\displaystyle={\cal H}^{q}({\rm gr}^{Q}_{k}M^{\bullet}_{l^{n+1}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n+1}}{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})

\displaystyle={\cal H}^{q}({\rm gr}^{Q}_{k}M^{\bullet}_{l^{n+1}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}))\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n+1}}{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}.

The last sheaf is isomorphic to

\begin{cases}0\quad(q\not=k)\\
{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}(-k)\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}c^{(k)}_{*}(\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}))\quad(q=k).\end{cases}

This is nothing but

{\cal H}^{q}({\rm gr}^{Q}_{k}M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}))
.
Because the filtration
Q
on
M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})
is biregular,
this means that

(M^{\bullet}_{l^{n+1}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}),Q)\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n+1}}{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}=(M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}),Q)

in
{\rm D}^{\rm b}{\rm F}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
.
By (
9.2.1
) the claim about the constructivility and the finite tor-dimension is obvious.
∎

Next let us recall the Rapoport-Zink-Nakayama’s
double complex (
[
RZ
]
,
[
Nak3
]
).

Let
I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}}

(n\in{\mathbb{N}})
be an injective resolution of
{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}

in
X_{\rm ket}
.
Then we have a projective system

\{I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}}\}_{n=1}^{\infty}
fitting into the following commutative diagram

(9.6.3)

\begin{CD}{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n+1}@>{}>{}>I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n+1}}\\
@V{{\rm proj}.}V{}V@V{}V{}V\\
{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}@>{}>{}>I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}}.\end{CD}

Set

(9.6.4)

K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}):=\epsilon_{X*}\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}*}\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}^{*}(I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}})\in C^{+}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}).

By (
9.6.3
) we have the projective system

\{K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\}_{n=1}^{\infty}
.

The following has been stated in
[
Nak3
, p. 723]
:

Proposition 9.7
.

Let
I^{\bullet}_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},l^{n}}
be an injective resolution of

{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}
in
(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})_{\rm ket}
.
Then the natural morphism

(9.7.1)

(\epsilon_{X}\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}})_{*}\pi^{*}_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}(I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}})\longrightarrow(\epsilon_{X}\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}})_{*}(I^{\bullet}_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},l^{n}}).

is a quasi-isomorphism. Consequently
the complex

K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})
is quasi-isomorphic to

(\epsilon_{X}\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}})_{*}(I^{\bullet}_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},l^{n}})
.

Proof.

Let
I^{\bullet}_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}},l^{n}}
be
an injective resolution of
{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}

in
(X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}})_{\rm ket}
for
m\in{\mathbb{N}}
.
Then we have the following natural morphism

(9.7.2)

\varinjlim_{m\in{\mathbb{N}}}(\epsilon_{X}\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}})_{*}(I^{\bullet}_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}},l^{n}})\longrightarrow(\epsilon_{X}\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}})_{*}(I^{\bullet}_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},l^{n}}).

This morphism is a quasi-isomorphism by

[
SGA 4-2
, VI (8.7.3.1)]
.
Since
X_{l^{m}}
is an object of the Kummer étale site of
X
,

\pi^{*}_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}}(I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}})
is injective
(
[
SGA 4-2
, V (4.11) (1)]
).
Hence we can set

I^{\bullet}_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}},l^{n}}:=\pi^{*}_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}}(I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}})

(m\in{\mathbb{N}})
.
In this case

(9.7.3)

\displaystyle\varinjlim_{m\in{\mathbb{N}}}(\epsilon_{X}\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}})_{*}(I^{\bullet}_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}},l^{n}})

\displaystyle=\varinjlim_{m\in{\mathbb{N}}}(\epsilon_{X}\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}})_{*}\pi^{*}_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}}(I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}})=\epsilon_{X*}\varinjlim_{m\in{\mathbb{N}}}\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}*}\pi^{*}_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}}(I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}})

\displaystyle=\epsilon_{X*}\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}*}\pi^{*}_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}(I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}}).

Here we have used
[
SGA 4-2
, VI (8.5.5)]
as in
[
Nak3
, p. 723]
.
Hence the morphism (
9.7.1
) is a quasi-isomorphism.
∎

Denote by
{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{m}(1)
the abelian sheaf in
\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et}
defined by the group scheme

\mu_{l^{m}}:=\underline{\rm Spec}_{\overset{\circ}{X}}({\cal O}_{X}[T_{m}]/(T^{l^{m}}_{m}-1))
.
Set
{\mathbb{Z}}_{l}(1):=\varprojlim_{n}{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{m}(1)
.
Fix a “generator”
T:=(T_{m})_{m\in{\mathbb{N}}}
of
{\mathbb{Z}}_{l}(1)
. Because

{\mathbb{Z}}_{l}(1)
acts on
X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}
naturally,
we have an endomorphism

(9.7.4)

T-1\colon\{K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\}_{n=1}^{\infty}\longrightarrow\{K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\}_{n=1}^{\infty}

of
\{K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\}_{n=1}^{\infty}
.
Let
{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)={\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)^{\bullet}

be the mapping fiber of this endomorphism:

(9.7.5)

\displaystyle{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)

\displaystyle:=K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\oplus K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})[-1]

\displaystyle=s((K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),d)\overset{T-1}{\longrightarrow}(K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),-d)).

Here
d
on the right hand side
is the boundary morphism of

K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})

and
s
means the single complex of a double complex.
Obviously we have the projective system

\{{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)\}_{n=1}^{\infty}
.

Let
\theta\colon\{{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)\}_{n=1}^{\infty}\longrightarrow\{{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(1)[1]\}_{n=1}^{\infty}
be
the following vertical morphism

(9.7.7)

\footnotesize{\begin{CD}\{(K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})(1),-d(1))\}_{n=1}^{\infty}@>{-(T-1)}>{}>\{(K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})(1),d(1))]\}_{n=1}^{\infty}\\
@A{{\rm id}\otimes T}A{}A\\
\{(K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),d)]\}_{n=1}^{\infty}@>{T-1}>{}>\{(K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),-d)]\}_{n=1}^{\infty}\end{CD}}

(
[
SaT
, (1.6)]
).

Lemma 9.8

(
[
Nak3
, p. 723]
).

The complex
{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)
is isomorphic to

R\epsilon_{X*}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
in
D^{+}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
.
In particular, if
q>>0
, then
{\cal H}^{q}({\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1))=0
for all
n\in{\mathbb{N}}
.

Proof.

By
[
Nak3
, (1.3.1)]
the following sequence

(9.8.1)

0\longrightarrow I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}}\longrightarrow\pi_{X_{l^{\infty}}*}\pi^{*}_{X_{l^{\infty}}}(I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}})\overset{T-1}{\longrightarrow}\pi_{X_{l^{\infty}_{*}}}\pi^{*}_{X_{l^{\infty}}}(I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}})\longrightarrow 0\\

is exact. Because
I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}}
is a complex of injective
{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}
-modules in

\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et}
, the sequence

(9.8.2)

0\longrightarrow\epsilon_{X*}(I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}})\longrightarrow(\epsilon_{X}\pi_{X_{l^{\infty}}})_{*}\pi^{*}_{X_{l^{\infty}}}(I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}})\overset{T-1}{\longrightarrow}(\epsilon_{X}\pi_{X_{l^{\infty}}})_{*}\pi^{*}_{X_{l^{\infty}}}(I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}})\longrightarrow 0\\

is exact. Hence
{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)
is quasi-isomorphic to

\epsilon_{X*}(I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}})
.
Consequently, by
[
KN
, (2.4)]
,

(9.8.3)

\displaystyle{\cal H}^{q}({\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1))

\displaystyle=R^{q}\epsilon_{X*}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})=\bigwedge^{q}(M_{X}^{\rm gp}/{\cal O}_{X}^{*})\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}(-q)

\displaystyle=a^{(q-1)}_{*}(({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(q-1)}}(-q)\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(q-1)}_{\rm et}(X/S)).

Because
\overset{\circ}{X}
is quasi-compact,

{\cal H}^{q}({\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1))=0

(
\exists q_{0}>0
,
\forall q>q_{0}
,
\forall n\in{\mathbb{N}}
).
∎

Corollary 9.9
.

The projective system

\{({\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1),\tau)\}_{n=1}^{\infty}
defines a well-defined object of

{\rm D}^{\rm b}{\rm F}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}_{l})
.

(
We denote the resulting filtered complex by

{(\rm MF}_{l^{\infty}}(T-1),\tau)
by abuse of notation.
)

Proof.

By using (
9.8.3
),
the proof is the same as that of (
9.6
).
∎

Denote also by
\theta
the following natural morphism

(9.9.1)

K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\owns x\longmapsto(0,x\otimes T)\in{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(1)[1].

Remark 9.10
.

The section
T
in
(0,x\otimes T)
in (
9.9.1
)
will turn out to be very important when we consider
the contravariant functoriality in (
11.3
) below.

Let
A^{\bullet\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})

be the double complex defined by
the following formula and
the following boundary morphisms;

(9.10.1)

A^{ij}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}):=({\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(j+1)/\tau_{j}{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(j+1))^{i+j+1}\quad(i\in{\mathbb{Z}},j\in{\mathbb{N}}),

(9.10.2)

\begin{CD}A^{i,j+1}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})@>{-d}>{}>A^{i+1,+1}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\\
@A{\theta}A{}A@A{}A{\theta}A\\
A^{ij}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})@>{-d}>{}>A^{i+1,j}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}).\end{CD}

Note that the signs of our boundary morphisms are different from that of
the boundary morphisms in the proof of
[
RZ
, p. 29]
and
[
Nak3
, the proof of (1.4)]
.
(We think that the signs in (
9.10.2
) are the best.)
Let
A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})
be the single complex of

A^{\bullet\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})
:

(9.10.3)

\displaystyle A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})=

\displaystyle s\{(({\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(1)/\tau_{0}{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(1))^{\bullet+1},-d)\overset{\theta}{\longrightarrow}

\displaystyle(({\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(2)/\tau_{1}{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(2))^{\bullet+2},-d)\overset{\theta}{\longrightarrow}\cdots\}.

Then we have the projective system

\{A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\}_{n=1}^{\infty}
and a morphism

\theta\colon\{K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\}_{n=1}^{\infty}\longrightarrow\{A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\}_{n=1}^{\infty}

of projective systems of complexes.

In the proof of
[
Nak3
, (1.4)]

Nakayama has proved that the morphism (
9.9.1
)
induces the following isomorphism

(9.10.4)

\displaystyle\theta\colon R(\epsilon_{X}\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}})_{*}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})

in
D^{+}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
.
Here we prove this fact by the same proof as that of
[
SZ
, (5.13)]

(and
[
FN
, (3.17)]
):

Proposition 9.11
.

The morphism

\theta\colon K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\longrightarrow{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(1)[1]

induces a quasi-isomorphism

(9.11.1)

\theta\colon K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\longrightarrow A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})

in
C^{+}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
.

Proof.

It is obvious that, for
i\geq 0
, the sequence

0\longrightarrow K^{i}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\overset{\theta}{\longrightarrow}A^{i0}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\overset{\theta}{\longrightarrow}A^{i1}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\overset{\theta}{\longrightarrow}\cdots

is exact.
We have to prove that
the complex
A^{-1\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})
is acyclic.
Consider the action of
T-1
on

R^{j}\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}*}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
.
Let
\overline{x}(\log)
be the log geometric point of
X
.
Then,
by the log proper base change theorem

[
Nak1
, (5.1)]
and
[
Nak1
, (4.1)]
,

R^{j}\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}*}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})_{\overline{x}(\log)}=H^{j}({\mathbb{Z}}_{l},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
.
This is equal to

{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}

(j=0,1)
and
0

(j\geq 2)
and the action of

{\mathbb{Z}}_{l}
on
{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}
is trivial.
Hence
T
acts trivially on

R^{j}\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}*}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})_{\overline{x}(\log)}
.
Consequently
T
acts trivially on
R^{j}\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}*}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
.
By the following Leray spectral sequence

E_{2}^{ij}=R^{i}\epsilon_{X*}R^{j}\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}*}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})\Longrightarrow R^{i+j}(\epsilon_{X}\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}})_{*}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}),

we see that
T
acts trivially on

{\cal H}^{q}(K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}))=R^{q}(\epsilon_{X}\pi_{X})_{*}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})

(q\in{\mathbb{N}})
.
Hence
T={\rm id}
on
{\cal H}^{q}(K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}))
.
Set
K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}:=K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})
.
Let
(x,y)
be a local section of
K_{l^{n}}^{j}(j+1)\oplus K_{l^{n}}^{j-1}(j+1)

(j\in{\mathbb{N}})
such that
\theta(x,y)=(0,x\otimes T)\in{\rm Ker}(d:{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)^{j+1}(j+2)\longrightarrow{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)^{j+2}(j+2))
. Then
dx=0
and hence

x
defines a local section of
{\cal H}^{j}(K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}})(j+1)
.
Because
T
acts trivially on

{\cal H}^{j}(K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}})
,
(T-1)(x)=0
.
Therefore there exists a local section
z\in K^{j-1}_{l^{n}}(j+1)

such that
(T-1)(x)=dz
. We immediately see that

d(x,z)=0
and
(x,y)=(x,z)+\theta((y-z)\otimes\check{T},0)\in{\rm Ker}\,d+{\rm Im}(\theta)
, where

\check{T}
is the dual basis of
T
.
The last formula tells us that
A^{-1\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})
is acyclic.

We complete the proof of (
9.11
).
∎

Corollary 9.12
.

The complex
A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\in D^{+}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}_{l})

is independent of the choice of
I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}}
.

For
i\in{\mathbb{Z}}
and
j\in{\mathbb{N}}
,
set

(9.12.1)

\{P_{k}A^{ij}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\}_{n=1}^{\infty}:=\{((\tau_{2j+k+1}+\tau_{j}){\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(j+1)/\tau_{j}{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(j+1))^{i+j+1}\}_{n=1}^{\infty}\quad

and

(9.12.2)

\displaystyle\{P_{k}A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\}_{n=1}^{\infty}:=\{s\{((\tau_{k+1}+\tau_{0}){\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(1)/\tau_{0}{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(1))^{\bullet+1},-d)\overset{\theta}{\longrightarrow}

\displaystyle\cdots\overset{\theta}{\longrightarrow}(\tau_{2j+k+1}+\tau_{j}){\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(j)/\tau_{j}{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(j))^{\bullet+j},-d)\overset{\theta}{\longrightarrow}\cdots\}\}_{n=1}^{\infty}.

(Note that
\tau_{2j+k+1}{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(j)/\tau_{j}{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(j)
in a lot of literatures
is incorrect since
2j+k+1<j
may occur.) We have a filtered complex

(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P)\in{\rm C}^{+}{\rm F}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
.

Proposition 9.13
.

If
k>>0
, then the natural inclusion

P_{k}A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})

is a quasi-isomorphism for all
n\in{\mathbb{N}}
.
If
k<<0
, then

P_{k}A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})

is exact for all
n\in{\mathbb{N}}
.

Proof.

First we prove the former statement.
Consider the following commutative diagram

(9.13.1)

\begin{CD}E_{1}^{pq}={\cal H}^{q}((\tau_{2p+k+1}+\tau_{p})({\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(p+1)/\tau_{p}{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(p+1))^{\bullet+1})\\
@V{}V{}V\\
E_{1}^{pq}={\cal H}^{q}(({\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(p+1)/\tau_{p}{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(p+1))^{\bullet+1})\\
\Longrightarrow{\cal H}^{p+q}(P_{k}A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}))\\
@V{}V{}V\\
\Longrightarrow{\cal H}^{p+q}(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})).\end{CD}

It suffices to prove that, for a fixed
p
, then

{\cal H}^{r}(({\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(p+1)/\tau_{p}{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(p+1))^{\bullet+1})=0

for
r>>0
.
By the following exact sequence

0\longrightarrow\tau_{p}{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)\longrightarrow{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)\longrightarrow{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)/\tau_{p}{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)\longrightarrow 0

and by (
9.8
),
it suffice to prove that

{\cal H}^{r}(\tau_{p}{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1))=0
if
r>>0

(\forall n\in{\mathbb{N}})
.
However this is obvious by (
9.8.3
).

The latter statement follows from (
9.8
) and (
9.8.3
)
since,
if
2j+k+1\geq j
and if
k<<0
,
then
j\geq-k>>0
in (
9.12.1
).
∎

Proposition 9.14
.

The filtered complex

(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P)\in{\rm D}^{+}{\rm F}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
is independent of the choice of
I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}}
.

Proof.

Let
k^{\prime}
be a large integer.
By (
9.13
) it suffices to prove that

(P_{k^{\prime}}A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),\{P_{k}\}_{k\leq k^{\prime}})\in{\rm D}^{+}{\rm F}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})

is independent of the choice of

I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}}
. Let
I^{\prime}{}^{\bullet}_{\!\!\!X,l^{n}}

be another injective resolution
of
{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}
in
X_{\rm ket}
.
Then we have analogous complexes

K^{\prime}{}^{\bullet}_{\!\!\!l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})
and
{\rm MF}^{\prime}_{l^{n}}(T-1)
as in
(
9.6.4
) and (
9.7.5
), respectively.
Since we have a quasi-isomorphism

I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}}\longrightarrow I^{\prime}{}^{\bullet}_{\!\!\!X,l^{n}}

fitting into the following commutative diagram

\begin{CD}{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}@>{}>{}>I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}}\\
\Big\|@V{}V{}V\\
{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}@>{}>{}>I^{\prime}{}^{\bullet}_{\!\!\!X,l^{n}},\end{CD}

we have natural morphisms

K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S)\longrightarrow K^{\prime\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S)
and

{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)\longrightarrow{\rm MF}^{\prime}_{l^{n}}(T-1)
.
In fact we have a filtered quasi-isomorphism

(9.14.1)

({\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1),\tau)\longrightarrow({\rm MF}^{\prime}_{l^{n}}(T-1),\tau)

By the definition of
P
, we have the following:

(9.14.2)

\displaystyle{\rm gr}_{k}^{P}A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})=\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k,0\}}{\rm gr}_{2j+k+1}^{\tau}{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(j)\{j\}[1]

because the induced morphism

\theta\colon{\rm gr}_{2j+k+1}^{\tau}{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(j)\{j\}[1]\longrightarrow{\rm gr}_{2(j+1)+k+1}^{\tau}{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(j+1)\{j+1\}[1]

is a zero morphism.
The isomorphism (
9.10.4
), (
9.13
),
(
9.8.3
) and the descending induction on
k
show (
9.14
).
∎

By (
9.8.3
) and (
9.14.2
) we have

(9.14.3)

\displaystyle{\rm gr}^{P}_{k}A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})

\displaystyle=\underset{j\geq{\rm max}\{-k,0\}}{\bigoplus}{\rm gr}^{\tau}_{2j+k+1}(A^{\bullet j}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\{-j\},-d)

\displaystyle=\underset{j\geq{\rm max}\{-k,0\}}{\bigoplus}{\cal H}^{2j+k+1}({\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1),-d)[-2j-k-1]\{1\}(j+1)

\displaystyle\simeq\underset{j\geq{\rm max}\{-k,0\}}{\bigoplus}R^{2j+k+1}\epsilon_{X*}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})[-2j-k](j+1)

\displaystyle=\underset{j\geq{\rm max}\{-k,0\}}{\bigoplus}{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}(-j-k)\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}a^{(2j+k)}_{*}(\varpi^{(2j+k)}_{\rm et}(X/S))[-2j-k]

in
D^{+}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
.
If
k>>0
, then
2j+k\geq k>>0
.
Because
\overset{\circ}{X}
is quasi-compact,

\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k)}=\emptyset
if
2j+k>>0
.
Hence

{\rm gr}^{P_{k}}A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})=0
if
k>>0

(\forall n\in{\mathbb{N}})
.
If
k<<0
, then
2j+k\geq-2k+k=-k>>0
.
Hence
\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k)}=\emptyset
and

{\rm gr}^{P}_{k}A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})=0

(\forall n\in{\mathbb{N}})
.
For a fixed
k\in{\mathbb{Z}}
, there are only finitely many
j
’s such that
the last term in (
9.14.3
) is nonzero.

Corollary 9.15
.

(1)
The filtered complexes

(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P)
’s
for
n
’s define a well-defined object

(A^{\bullet}_{l^{\infty}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P)
of

{\rm D}^{\rm b}{\rm F}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}_{l})
.

(2)
The filtered complexes
K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})
’s for
n
’s
define a well-defined object of

K^{\bullet}_{l^{\infty}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})
of

D^{\rm b}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}_{l})
.

Proof.

(1):
As in (
9.6
), this follows from (
9.13
) and (
9.9
)
and the definition of
(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P)
.
Indeed, let
\{(F^{\bullet}_{n},\tau)\}_{n=1}^{\infty}
be
a representative of the resulting filtered complex
obtained in (
9.9
).
The projective system of

\displaystyle\{(A_{n}^{\bullet},P)\}_{n=1}^{\infty}:=\{(s(F^{\bullet}_{n}/\tau_{0}\longrightarrow F^{\bullet}_{n}/\tau_{1}\longrightarrow\cdots),\{s(\cdots(\tau_{2j+k+1}+\tau_{j})/\tau_{j}F_{n}^{i+j+1}\cdots)_{i\in{\mathbb{Z}},j\in{\mathbb{N}}}\}_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}})\}_{n=1}^{\infty}

defines a well-defined object of

{\rm D}^{\rm b}{\rm F}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}_{l})
.

(2): (2) follows from (1) and (
9.11
).
∎

Henceforth we take representatives of

K^{\bullet}_{l^{\infty}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})=\{K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\}_{n=1}^{\infty}\in{\rm D}^{\rm b}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}_{l})

in
\varprojlim_{n}C^{\rm b}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})

and

(A^{\bullet}_{l^{\infty}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P)=\{(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P)\}_{n=1}^{\infty}\in{\rm D}^{\rm b}{\rm F}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}_{l})
in

\varprojlim_{n}{\rm C}^{\rm b}{\rm F}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
,
respectively.
By abuse of notation, we use the same symbols for them:

K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\in C^{\rm b}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})~{\textrm{a}}{\textrm{n}}{\textrm{d}}~(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P)\in{\rm C}^{\rm b}{\rm F}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}).

Consider the following filtered complex

(K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}})
:

(K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}):=s(K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}}(M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}),Q))\in{\rm C}^{\rm b}{\rm F}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}).

Here
K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})
means the filtered complex

K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})
with trivial filtration by abuse of notation.
Obviously we have the projective system

\{(K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}})\}_{n=1}^{\infty}
.

Proposition 9.16
.

(9.16.1)

\{R(\epsilon_{(X,D)}\pi_{(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})})_{*}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})\}_{n=1}^{\infty}=\{K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\}_{n=1}^{\infty}

in
D^{\rm b}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}_{l})
.

Proof.

Let
\epsilon\colon(X,D)\longrightarrow(\overset{\circ}{X},\overset{\circ}{D})
be the natural morphism of log schemes.
Consider the following cartesian diagram:

(9.16.2)

\begin{CD}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}})@>{\epsilon\circ\pi_{(X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}})}}>{}>(\overset{\circ}{X},\overset{\circ}{D})\\
@V{}V{}V@V{}V{\overset{\circ}{\epsilon}_{D}}V\\
X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}@>{\epsilon_{X}\circ\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}}}>{}>\overset{\circ}{X}.\end{CD}

Then, by the finiteness of the cohomological dimension
(
[
Nak4
, (7.2) (1)]
) and the log Künneth formula
(
[
Nak1
, (6.1)]
), we obtain the following

(9.16.3)

\displaystyle R(\epsilon_{(X,D)}\pi_{(X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}})})_{*}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})

\displaystyle=R(\epsilon_{X}\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}})_{*}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})\otimes^{L}_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}}R\overset{\circ}{\epsilon}_{D*}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})

\displaystyle=(\epsilon_{X}\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}})_{*}(I^{\bullet}_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}},l^{n}})\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}}M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})

\displaystyle=(\epsilon_{X}\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}})_{*}(\pi^{*}_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}}(I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}}))\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}}M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})

in
D^{+}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
.
Taking the inductive limit of (
9.16.3
) with respect to
m

and using the
quasi-isomorphism (
9.7.2
),
we have the formula (
9.16.1
).
∎

Consider the following double complex and the single complex:

(9.16.4)

(A^{\bullet\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}):=A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}}(M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}),Q)

and

(9.16.5)

(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}):=s(A^{\bullet\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}})\in{\rm C}^{\rm b}{\rm F}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}).

Here
A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})
means the filtered complex

A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})
with trivial filtration by abuse of notation.

Consider also the following filtered double complex and the single complex:

(9.16.6)

(A^{\bullet\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P):=(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P)\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}}(M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}),Q)

and

(9.16.7)

(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P):=s(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P)\in{\rm C}^{\rm b}{\rm F}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}).

Then we obtain the following bifiltered complex

(9.16.8)

(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}},P):=(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}},P)\in{\rm C}^{\rm b}{\rm F}^{2}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}).

Obviously we have the projective system

\{(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}},P)\}_{n=1}^{\infty}
.

Proposition 9.17
.

The morphism

\theta\otimes 1\colon K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})=K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}}M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})\longrightarrow{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(1)[1]\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}}M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})

induces a filtered quasi-isomorphism

(9.17.1)

\theta\otimes 1\colon(K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}})\longrightarrow(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}})

in
{\rm C}^{+}{\rm F}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
.

Proof.

Because
(M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}),Q)
is
a filtered flat complex of
{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}
-modules in
\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et}
,
(
9.17
) follows from (
9.11
).
∎

The bifiltered complex (
9.16.8
) defines an object
of
{\rm D}^{\rm b}{\rm F}^{2}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
,
By abuse of notation, we denote it
by

(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}},P)
.

Proposition 9.18
.

There exists an isomorphism

(9.18.1)

R(\epsilon_{(X,D)}\pi_{(X_{{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})})_{*}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})

in
D^{\rm b}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
.

Proof.

(
9.18
) immediately follows from
(
9.16
) and (
9.17
).
∎

The following is a main result in this section.

Theorem 9.19
.

(1)
The bifiltered complex

(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}},P)\in{\rm D}^{\rm b}{\rm F}^{2}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})

is independent of the choices of
I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}}
and

(M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}),Q)
.
This bifiltered complex is also independent of the choice of
T
.
This is an object of
{\rm D}^{\rm b}{\rm F}^{2}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
.

(2)

\displaystyle(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n+1}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}},P)\otimes^{L}_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n+1}}{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}=(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}},P)

in
{\rm D}^{\rm b}{\rm F}^{2}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
.
Here
\otimes^{L}_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n+1}}
is the derived tensor product of bounded below
bifiltered complexes defined in
(
6.2.8
)
.

Proof.

Let
({\cal T},{\cal A})
be a ringed topos. Let

L^{0}\colon\{{\cal A}\text{-modules}\}\longrightarrow\{\text{flat }{\cal A}\text{-modules}\}

be the the sheafification of the presheaf

U\longmapsto\text{free }\Gamma(U,{\cal A})\text{-module
with basis }\Gamma(U,E)\setminus\{0\}.

First we fix a generator
T
of
{\mathbb{Z}}_{l}(1)
.
Let
I^{\prime\bullet}_{X,l^{n}}
be another injective resolution
of
{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}
in
X_{\rm ket}
as in the proof of (
9.14
).
Let
{\rm MF}^{\prime}_{l^{n}}(T-1)
be the complex in the proof of (
9.14
).
Let
(M^{\prime\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}),Q^{\prime})
be an analogous filtered flat complex to

(M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}),Q)
.
Using
L^{0}
, we see that
there exists an analogous filtered flat complex

(M^{\prime\prime\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})),Q^{\prime\prime})
to

(M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})),Q)

such that there exist the following filtered quasi-isomorphisms:

(9.19.1)

\displaystyle(M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}),Q)\overset{\sim}{\longleftarrow}(M^{\prime\prime\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}),Q^{\prime\prime})\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}(M^{\prime\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}),Q^{\prime}).

Hence we may assume that there exists a filtered quasi-isomorphism

(9.19.2)

\displaystyle(M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}),Q)\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}(M^{\prime\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}),Q^{\prime}).

Because the bifiltrations

P
and
P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}
are biregular,
we have only to prove that

{\rm gr}_{k^{\prime}}^{P}{\rm gr}_{k}^{P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}}A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})
is independent of the choice of

I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}}
and
(M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}),Q)

(cf. the proof of (
2.13
)).
Since

{\rm gr}^{Q}_{k}M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})

is a complex of flat
{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}
-modules,
we have the following formula

(9.19.3)

\displaystyle{\rm gr}_{k^{\prime}}^{P}{\rm gr}_{k}^{P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}}A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})

\displaystyle={\rm gr}_{k^{\prime}}^{P}(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}}{\rm gr}^{Q}_{k}M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}))

\displaystyle=\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}{\rm gr}_{2j+k^{\prime}+1-k}^{\tau}{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(j)\{j\}[1]\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}}{\rm gr}^{Q}_{k}M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})

by
[
NS
, (1.2.4) (2)]
.
Since

{\rm gr}^{Q}_{k}M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})

is a complex of flat
{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}
-modules,
the following morphism

\displaystyle{\rm gr}_{2j+k^{\prime}+1-k}^{\tau}{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(j)\{j\}[1]\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}}{\rm gr}^{Q}_{k}M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})

\displaystyle\longrightarrow({\rm gr}_{2j+k^{\prime}+1-k}^{\tau}{\rm MF}^{\prime}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(j)\{j\}[1]\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}}{\rm gr}^{Q}_{k}M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})

is a quasi-isomorphism by (
9.8.3
).
Furthermore,
the following morphism

\displaystyle{\rm gr}_{2j+k^{\prime}+1-k}^{\tau}{\rm MF}^{\prime}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(j)\{j\}[1]\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}}{\rm gr}^{Q}_{k}M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})

\displaystyle\longrightarrow({\rm gr}_{2j+k^{\prime}+1-k}^{\tau}{\rm MF}^{\prime}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(j)\{j\}[1]\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}}{\rm gr}^{Q^{\prime}}_{k}M^{\prime\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})

is a quasi-isomorphism by (
9.6.1
).
Now we have proved the independence of the choices of
I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}}
and

(M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}),Q)
.

The independence of the choice of
T
follows from (
9.8.2
).

By (
9.4
), (
9.8.3
), (
9.14.3
) and (
9.19.3
),
we see that the claim about
the constructivility and the finite tor-dimension holds.

(2): Obviously we have a natural morphism

\displaystyle(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n+1}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}},P)\otimes^{L}_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n+1}}{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}\longrightarrow(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}},P).

Let
R^{\bullet}
be a flat resolution of

{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}
of
{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n+1}
-modules.
Then

(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n+1}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}},P)\otimes^{L}_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n+1}}{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}=(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n+1}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}},P)\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n+1}}R^{\bullet}
.
As in (1), it suffices to prove that

\displaystyle{\rm gr}_{k^{\prime}}^{P}{\rm gr}_{k}^{P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}}\{(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n+1}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}},P)\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n+1}}R^{\bullet}\}

\displaystyle={\rm gr}_{k^{\prime}}^{P}{\rm gr}_{k}^{P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}}(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}},P).

This is obtained as follows:

\displaystyle{\rm gr}_{k^{\prime}}^{P}{\rm gr}_{k}^{P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}}\{(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n+1}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}},P)\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n+1}}R^{\bullet}\}

\displaystyle={\rm gr}_{k^{\prime}}^{P}{\rm gr}_{k}^{P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}}(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n+1}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}},P)\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n+1}}R^{\bullet}

\displaystyle=\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}{\rm gr}_{2j+k^{\prime}+1-k}^{\tau}{\rm MF}_{l^{n+1}}(T-1)(j)\{j\}[1]\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n+1}}{\rm gr}^{Q}_{k}M^{\bullet}_{l^{n+1}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n+1}}R^{\bullet}

\displaystyle\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}{\rm gr}_{2j+k^{\prime}+1-k}^{\tau}{\rm MF}_{l^{n}}(T-1)(j)\{j\}[1]\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}}{\rm gr}^{Q}_{k}M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}).

Here, to obtain the last quasi-isomorphism, we have used (
9.8.3
) and (
9.14.3
).
∎

Definition 9.20
.

We call the bifiltered complex

(A^{\bullet}_{l^{\infty}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}},P):=\{(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}},P)\}_{n=1}^{\infty}\in{\rm D}^{\rm b}{\rm F}^{2}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}_{l})

the
l
-
adic bifiltered El Zein-Steenbrink-Zucker complex
of
(X,D)

over
S
. We call
P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}
and
P

the weight filtration with respect
to
D

and

the weight filtration
on

A^{\bullet}_{l^{\infty}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})
, respectively.
(We do not use a traditional notation

W
in this paper because
we have to use the symbol
W

for the Witt ring in other papers,
e. g.,
[
M
]
,
[
Nakk1
]
.)

We also have the following objects

\displaystyle{\rm gr}_{k}^{P^{\overset{\circ}{D}}}A^{\bullet}_{l^{\infty}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}):=\{{\rm gr}_{k}^{P^{\overset{\circ}{D}}}A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\}_{n=1}^{\infty}\in D^{\rm b}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}_{l})

and

\displaystyle{\rm gr}_{k}^{P}A^{\bullet}_{l^{\infty}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}):=\{{\rm gr}_{k}^{P}A^{\bullet}_{l^{\infty}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\}_{n=1}^{\infty}\in D^{\rm b}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}_{l}).

Remark 9.21
.

Assume that
\overset{\circ}{S}
is regular of dimension
\leq 1
.
Let
f\colon X\longrightarrow S
be the structural morphism.
Let
{\rm D}^{\rm bb}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})

and

{\rm D}^{\rm bb}{\rm F}^{2}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})

be the derived category of bounded biregular complexes of

{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}
-modules
in
\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et}
and
the derived category of bounded biregular bifiltered complexes of

{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}
-modules.
Let
{\rm D}^{\rm bb}{\rm F}^{2}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}_{l})

be the projective 2-limit of

{\rm D}^{\rm bb}{\rm F}^{2}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
.
As remarked in
[
D5
, (1.1.2) c)]
,

{\rm D}^{\rm bb}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})

is stable under

R\overset{\circ}{f}_{*}
,
\overset{\circ}{f}{}^{*}
,
R\overset{\circ}{f}_{!}
,
R\overset{\circ}{f}{}^{!}
,

\otimes^{L}
and
{\rm RHom}^{\bullet}
and
commutes with reduction mod
l^{n}{\mathbb{Z}}
.
Hence so is for

{\rm D}^{\rm bb}{\rm F}^{2}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}_{l})
.
Because
(A^{\bullet}_{l^{\infty}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}},P)\in{\rm D}^{\rm bb}{\rm F}^{2}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}_{l})
,
we can define

R\overset{\circ}{f}_{*}((A^{\bullet}_{l^{\infty}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}},P))\in{\rm D}^{\rm bb}{\rm F}^{2}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{S}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}_{l}).

10
l
-adic weight spectral sequences

In this section we give generalizations of
(
1.1.3
) and (
1.1.4
).

Proposition 10.1
.

Let
\overset{\circ}{Z}
be a closed SNC scheme of

\overset{\circ}{X}
over
\overset{\circ}{S}
.
Set
Z:=X\times_{\overset{\circ}{X}}\overset{\circ}{Z}
.
Assume that
Z
is an SNCL scheme over
S
.
Let
\iota\colon Z\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}X
and

\iota_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}\colon Z_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}\overset{\subset}{\longrightarrow}X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}

be the natural closed immersions.
Let
I^{\bullet}_{Z,l^{n}}
be an injective resolution of

{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}
in
Z_{\rm ket}
.
Set

K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(Z_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}):=\epsilon_{Z*}\pi_{Z_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}*}\pi_{Z_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}^{*}(I^{\bullet}_{Z,l^{n}})\in C^{+}(\overset{\circ}{Z}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
.
Then the natural morphism

\overset{\circ}{\iota}{}^{*}(K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}))\longrightarrow K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(Z_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})

induced by a natural morphism

\iota^{*}(I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}})\longrightarrow I^{\bullet}_{Z,l^{n}}

is a quasi-isomorphism fitting into the following commutative diagram
:

\begin{CD}\overset{\circ}{\iota}{}^{*}(K^{\bullet}_{l^{n+1}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}))@>{}>{}>K^{\bullet}_{l^{n+1}}(Z_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\\
@V{}V{}V@V{}V{}V\\
\overset{\circ}{\iota}{}^{*}(K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}))@>{}>{}>K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(Z_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}).\end{CD}

Proof.

Consider the following two cartesian diagrams:

\begin{CD}Z_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}@>{\subset}>{}>X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}\\
@V{\pi_{Z_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}}}V{}V@V{}V{\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}}}V\\
Z@>{\subset}>{}>X\\
@V{\epsilon_{Z}}V{}V@V{}V{\epsilon_{X}}V\\
\overset{\circ}{Z}@>{\subset}>{}>\overset{\circ}{X}.\end{CD}

In
D^{\rm b}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
,

(\epsilon_{X}\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}})_{*}\pi^{*}_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}}(I^{\bullet}_{X,l^{n}})
is equal to

R(\epsilon_{X}\pi_{X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}})_{*}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
.
Now (
10.1
) follows from the quasi-isomorphisms (
9.7.1
), (
9.7.2
) and
the log proper base change theorem of
Nakayama (
[
Nak1
, (5.1)]
).
(It is easy to check the condition on the charts of the log structures of

X_{\frac{1}{l^{m}}}
,
X
and
Z
in [loc. cit.] is satisfied.)
∎

Proposition 10.2
.

Let the notations be as in
(
10.1
)
.
Then

\overset{\circ}{\iota}{}^{*}((A^{\bullet}_{l^{\infty}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P))=(A^{\bullet}_{l^{\infty}}(Z_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P)

in
D^{\rm b}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{Z}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}_{l})
.

Proof.

(
10.2
)
follows from (
10.1
) and
the following commutative diagram:

\begin{CD}\overset{\circ}{\iota}{}^{*}(K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}))@>{T-1}>{}>\overset{\circ}{\iota}{}^{*}(K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}))\\
@V{}V{}V@V{}V{}V\\
K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(Z_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})@>{T-1}>{}>K^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(Z_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}).\end{CD}

∎

Lemma 10.3
.

Let
k
be a nonnegative integer.
Then the following hold
:

(1)
There exists an isomorphism

(10.3.1)

{\rm gr}_{k}^{P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}}A^{\bullet}_{l^{\infty}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}c^{(k)}_{*}(A^{\bullet}_{l^{\infty}}(D^{(k)}_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})(-k)\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}))\{-k\}

in
D^{\rm b}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}_{l})
.

(2)
There exists an isomorphism

(10.3.2)

\displaystyle{\rm gr}_{k}^{P}A^{\bullet}_{l^{\infty}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})

\displaystyle\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}\{\bigoplus^{k}_{k^{\prime}=-\infty}\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}a^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k-k^{\prime})}_{*}(({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}}

\displaystyle\quad\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k-k^{\prime})}_{\rm et}((\overset{\circ}{X},\overset{\circ}{D})/\overset{\circ}{S}))(-j-k)[-2j-k]\}_{n}

in
D^{\rm b}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}_{l})
.

(3)
There exists an isomorphism

(10.3.3)

\displaystyle{\rm gr}_{k}^{P}{\rm gr}_{k^{\prime}}^{P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}}A^{\bullet}_{l^{\infty}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})={\rm gr}_{k^{\prime}}^{P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}}{\rm gr}_{k}^{P}A^{\bullet}_{l^{\infty}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}

\displaystyle\{\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-(k-k^{\prime}),0\}}a^{(2j+k-k^{\prime}),(k^{\prime})}_{*}(({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k-k^{\prime})}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k^{\prime})}}{}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(2j+k-k^{\prime}),(k^{\prime})}_{\rm et}((\overset{\circ}{X},\overset{\circ}{D})/\overset{\circ}{S}))

\displaystyle(-j-k)[-2j-k]\}_{n}

in
D^{\rm b}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}_{l})
.

Proof.

(1): Since
(M^{q}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}),Q)
is
a filtered flat
{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}
-module in
\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et}
,

{\rm gr}^{Q}_{k}(M^{q}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}))
is
a flat
{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}
-module in
\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et}
.
Hence we have the following equalities:

(10.3.4)

\displaystyle{\rm gr}_{k}^{P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}}A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}){}

\displaystyle=s(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}}{\rm gr}_{k}^{Q}M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}))

\displaystyle=A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}c^{(k)}_{*}(\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}))(-k)\{-k\}

\displaystyle=c^{(k)}_{*}(c^{(k)*}(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})(-k))\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}))\{-k\}

\displaystyle=c^{(k)}_{*}(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(D^{(k)}_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})(-k)\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}))\{-k\}.

Here, to obtain the second equality (resp. the last equality),
we have used (
9.2.1
) (resp. (
10.2
)).
The compatibility with respect to
n
is obvious.
We complete the proof of (1).

(2): We have the following equalities:

(10.3.5)

\displaystyle{\rm gr}_{k}^{P}A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})

\displaystyle=\bigoplus^{k}_{k^{\prime}=-\infty}\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}({\rm gr}_{2j+k^{\prime}+1}^{\tau}A^{\bullet j}_{l^{n}}(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})[-j],-d)\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}}{\rm gr}^{Q}_{k-k^{\prime}}M^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})

\displaystyle=\bigoplus^{k}_{k^{\prime}=-\infty}\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})(-j-k^{\prime})\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}}a^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{*}(\varpi^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{X}/\overset{\circ}{S}))

\displaystyle\quad[-2j-k^{\prime}]\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})(-(k-k^{\prime}))\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}}c^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{*}(\varpi^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})))[-(k-k^{\prime})]

\displaystyle=\bigoplus^{k}_{k^{\prime}=-\infty}\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})(-j-k)\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}}a^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{*}(\varpi^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{X}/\overset{\circ}{S}))

\displaystyle\quad\otimes_{{\mathbb{Z}}}c^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{*}(\varpi^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}))[-2j-k].

Here, to obtain the first equality and the second one,
we have used
[
NS
, (1.2.4) (2)]
and the formula (
9.14.3
), respectively.
Hence we obtain (
10.3.2
) by (
9.1.4
).

(3): The proof of (3) is similar to that of (2).
∎

Because

(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}},P)\in{\rm D}^{\rm b}{\rm F}^{2}_{\rm ctf}(\overset{\circ}{X}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
,
we can consider

R\overset{\circ}{f}_{*}(A^{\bullet}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}},P)\in{\rm D}^{\rm b}{\rm F}^{2}(\overset{\circ}{S}_{\rm et},{\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}).

Corollary 10.4
.

Let
f_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(k)}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k^{\prime})}/\overset{\circ}{S}}\colon\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(k)}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k^{\prime})}\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{S}

(k,k^{\prime}\in{\mathbb{N}})

be the structural morphism.
Assume that
R^{q}f_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(k)}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k^{\prime})}/\overset{\circ}{S}*}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(k),(k^{\prime})}_{\rm et}((\overset{\circ}{X},\overset{\circ}{D})/\overset{\circ}{S}))
has finite stalks.
Let
f_{(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}\colon(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{S}
and

f_{D^{(k)}_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}\colon D^{(k)}_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}\longrightarrow\overset{\circ}{S}

be the structural morphisms.
Then the following hold
:

(1)

There exists the following spectral sequence
:

(10.4.1)

E_{1}^{-k,q+k}=R^{q-k}f_{D^{(k)}_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}*}({\mathbb{Z}}_{l}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\pi^{*}_{D^{(k)}_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}\epsilon^{*}_{D^{(k)}}(\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})))(-k)\Longrightarrow R^{q}f_{(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}*}({\mathbb{Z}}_{l}).

Here
R^{q-k}f_{D^{(k)}_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}*}({\mathbb{Z}}_{l}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\pi^{*}_{D^{(k)}_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}\epsilon^{*}_{D^{(k)}}(\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})))(-k):=\varprojlim_{n}R^{q-k}f_{D^{(k)}_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}*}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\pi^{*}_{D^{(k)}_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}\epsilon^{*}_{D^{(k)}}(\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})))(-k)

and

R^{q}f_{(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}*}({\mathbb{Z}}_{l}):=\varprojlim_{n}R^{q}f_{(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}*}({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})
.

(2)

There exists the following spectral sequence
:

(10.4.2)

\displaystyle E_{1}^{-k,q+k}

\displaystyle=\bigoplus^{k}_{k^{\prime}=-\infty}\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}R^{q-2j-k}f_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}/\overset{\circ}{S}*}({\mathbb{Z}}_{l}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}

\displaystyle\varpi^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k-k^{\prime})}_{\rm et}((\overset{\circ}{X},\overset{\circ}{D})/\overset{\circ}{S}))(-j-k)

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow R^{q}f_{(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}*}({\mathbb{Z}}_{l}).

(3)

There exists the following spectral sequence for
k^{\prime}\in{\mathbb{N}}:

(10.4.3)

\displaystyle E_{1}^{-k,q+k}

\displaystyle=\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k,0\}}R^{q-2j-k}f_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k)}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k^{\prime})}/\overset{\circ}{S}*}({\mathbb{Z}}_{l}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(2j+k),(k^{\prime})}_{\rm et}((\overset{\circ}{X},\overset{\circ}{D})/\overset{\circ}{S}))(-j-k)

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow R^{q}f_{D^{(k^{\prime})}_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}*}({\mathbb{Z}}_{l}).

Proof.

(First we prove (2).)
(2):
We obtain the following identification of cohomologies:

(10.4.4)

\displaystyle R^{q}\overset{\circ}{f}_{*}({\rm gr}_{k}^{P}A^{\bullet j}_{l^{n}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}))

\displaystyle\overset{R^{q}\overset{\circ}{f}_{*}((\ref{eqn:axd}))}{{\overset{\sim}{\longrightarrow}}}R^{q}\overset{\circ}{f}_{*}(\bigoplus^{k}_{k^{\prime}=-\infty}\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}a^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k-k^{\prime})}_{*}(({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}})(-j-k)

\displaystyle\quad\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}a^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{*}(\varpi^{(2j+k^{\prime})}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{X}/\overset{\circ}{S}))\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}c^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{*}(\varpi^{(k-k^{\prime})}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S}))[-2j-k])

\displaystyle=R^{q-2j-k}\overset{\circ}{f}_{*}(\bigoplus^{k}_{k^{\prime}=-\infty}\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}a^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k-k^{\prime})}_{*}(({\mathbb{Z}}/l^{n})_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}}

\displaystyle\quad\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(2j+k^{\prime}),(k-k^{\prime})}_{\rm et}((\overset{\circ}{X},\overset{\circ}{D})/\overset{\circ}{S}))(-j-k).

Hence the projective system of
E_{1}
-terms of (
10.4.2
)
with respect to
n
satisfies the Mittag-Leffler condition by the assumption of the finite stalks.
Taking the projective limit of (

)
with respect to

,
we obtain the spectral sequence (
10.4.2
).

(1): By (2),

R^{q}f_{D^{(k^{\prime})}_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}*}({\mathbb{Z}}_{l}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\pi^{*}_{D^{(k^{\prime})}_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}\epsilon^{*}_{D^{(k^{\prime})}}(\varpi^{(k^{\prime})}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{D}/\overset{\circ}{S})))
has finite stalks.
We immediately obtain (

)
by taking the projective limit of
the spectral sequence
obtained by (

) (1).

(3): The proof of (3) is the same as that of (2).
∎

Definition 10.5
.

) and (

)
the

(10.5.1)

\displaystyle P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}_{q+k}R^{q}f_{(X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}*}({\mathbb{Z}}_{l})

\displaystyle:={\rm Im}(R^{q}f_{*}(P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}_{k}A^{\bullet}_{l^{\infty}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}))\longrightarrow R^{q}f_{*}(A^{\bullet}_{l^{\infty}}((X_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})/S_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}})))

We call
P^{D_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}
and

the

Example 10.6
.

Assume that the relative dimension of
\overset{\circ}{X}
over

is of pure dimension

.
Then the

-terms of (

) with

are as follows:

\footnotesize{\begin{CD}\oplus_{i+j=1,i,j\geq 0}R^{0}f_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(i)}\cap\overset{\circ}{D}{}^{(j)}/\overset{\circ}{S}*}({\mathbb{Z}}_{l}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\varpi^{(i),(j)}_{\rm et}((\overset{\circ}{X},\overset{\circ}{D})/\overset{\circ}{S}))(-1)@>{}>{}>R^{2}f_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(0)}/\overset{\circ}{S}*}({\mathbb{Z}}_{l})\\
R^{1}f_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(0)}/\overset{\circ}{S}*}({\mathbb{Z}}_{l})\\
R^{0}f_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(0)}/\overset{\circ}{S}*}({\mathbb{Z}}_{l})@>{}>{}>R^{0}f_{\overset{\circ}{X}{}^{(1)}/\overset{\circ}{S}*}({\mathbb{Z}}_{l}).\end{CD}}

Let
s
be the log point of a field
\kappa
.
Let
\kappa_{\rm sep}
be a separable closure of
\kappa
and
set
\overline{s}:=s\otimes_{\kappa}{\kappa}_{\rm sep}
.
Let

be an SNCL over

with a relative SNCD

on

.
Set

.
The Galois group

acts on

and hence on

It is easy to see that

.
By (

(10.6.1)

E_{1}^{-k,q+k}=H^{q-k}(\overline{E}^{(k)}_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},{\mathbb{Z}}_{l}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}\pi^{*}_{\overline{E}{}^{(k)}_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}}\epsilon^{*}_{\overline{E}{}^{(k)}}(\varpi^{(k)}_{\rm et}(\overset{\circ}{E}/\overset{\circ}{s})))(-k)\Longrightarrow H^{q}((\overline{Y}_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},\overline{E}_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),{\mathbb{Z}}_{l}),

(10.6.2)

\displaystyle E_{1}^{-k,q+k}

\displaystyle=\bigoplus^{k}_{k^{\prime}=-\infty}\bigoplus_{j\geq\max\{-k^{\prime},0\}}H^{q-2j-k}((\overset{\circ}{\overline{Y}}{}^{(2j+k^{\prime})}\cap\overset{\circ}{\overline{E}}{}^{(k-k^{\prime})}),{\mathbb{Z}}_{l}\otimes_{\mathbb{Z}}

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow H^{q}((\overline{Y}_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}},\overline{E}_{\frac{1}{l^{\infty}}}),{\mathbb{Z}}_{l}),

These are spectral sequences of
